# ISF-REDUCTION-DELIVERY: SPECFORGE scalar and grouped reductions

## Metadata

- Tree ID: `ISF-REDUCTION-DELIVERY`
- Status: `active`
- Roadmap lane: `R14 / ISF; R13 public semantic reports`
- Created: `2026-10-21`
- Last updated: `2026-10-26`
- Owner: repo-local workflow

## Goal

Implement and qualify the required R1–R3 of SPECFORGE FSMGEN-REQUESTS.12.
SEMANTIC-INTROSPECTION-MCP-FRONTIER `.92` retains request coordination and
publication ownership. Decision `0117` selects the contract. This bounded
implementation tree avoids growing the near-rollover coordination task file.
Decision `0121` applies the director's exclusive required-request priority;
independent defects remain owned after handoff unless they demonstrably block delivery.

Director DBIQNP discussion, 2026-10-23: reaffirm the long-term goal of an external
agent authoring, simulating at VIAL level, debugging, fixing and iterating through
production-quality semantic API/MCP contracts, including precise temporal
behavior. This is discussion, not an activation or delivery claim. Existing R13,
MCP write-horizon and HIAL/VIAL owners retain that scope; the reduction frontier
and decision0121 priority do not change. Design meaning, observed execution and
evidence-backed causality must remain distinguishable.

Director instruction, 2026-10-23: implement POD-based FSMGen API reference
generation now, following the HTMLBatch/mdBook discussion. This explicitly
selects the documentation task ahead of the saved .2.3.2.4.2 implementation;
SPECFORGE priorities and publication gates resume afterward. The constructor
checkpoint is committed before activating that task. The 2026-10-25 instruction
returns exclusive priority to required SPECFORGE implementation; the prior POD
detour is complete. After each feature slice use only small focused checks and
mandatory commit doctrines. Defer full verification to publication readiness;
do not start broad suites between implementation slices.

## Non-Goals

- This tree does not close durations, named timers or record-output delivery.
- Optional inversion has separate ownership and cannot delay required delivery.
- No sibling repository, submodule pin or caller integration is changed here.

## Acceptance Criteria

- Every required request clause has source, IAL0, HDL and public-report evidence.
- Preserve accepted binary expressions and existing logical negation.
- Keep authored dependencies distinct from resolved defaults; prove actual HDL
  width and group behavior under parameter overrides and short final groups.
- Each completed source slice has a small focused check, synchronized book and
  task evidence, and its own commit. Full corpus/native/API/MCP and broad
  regression qualification are deferred to publication readiness under the
  director's 2026-10-25 instruction; feature completeness remains the priority.
- Full CI and exact-SHA hosted results qualify publication under SEM `.69`.
- Task integrity and all required doctrines pass without relaxed safety limits.
